matches {{key}} and {{ key }}\n        patterns[key] = re.compile(r'\\{\\{\\s*' + re.escape(key) + r'\\s*\\}\\}')\n    return patterns\n\n\ndef replace_in_section_xml(xml_text: str, data: dict, patterns: dict) -> tuple[str, int, int]:\n    \"\"\"Return (new_xml, num_replacements, num_paras_cache_removed).\"\"\"\n    root = ET.fromstring(xml_text)\n\n    replacements = 0\n    cache_removed = 0\n\n    for p in root.findall('.//hp:p', NS):\n        # Determine whether this paragraph *originally* contains placeholders\n        # (placeholders live in hp:t text in this template)\n        t_nodes = p.findall('.//hp:t', NS)\n        joined = ''.join((t.text or '') for t in t_nodes)\n        had_placeholder = '{{' in joined\n\n        for t in t_nodes:\n            if not t.text:\n                continue\n            before = t.text\n            after = before\n            for key, val in data.items():\n                after = patterns[key].sub(str(val), after)\n            if after != before:\n                # Count approximate replacements by token occurrences in the original text\n                # (not perfect, but good enough for logging)\n                for key in data.keys():\n                    replacements += len(patterns[key].findall(before))\n                t.text = after\n\n        if had_placeholder:\n            # Remove stale layout cache elements from modified paragraphs only\n            # Exact tag observed in the template: hp:linesegarray\n            removed_any = False\n            for child in list(p):\n                if child.tag == qname('linesegarray'):\n                    p.remove(child)\n                    removed_any = True\n            if removed_any:\n                cache_removed += 1\n\n    new_xml = ET.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True).decode('utf-8')\n    return new_xml, replacements, cache_removed\n\n\ndef main():\n    data = load_data(JSON_PATH)\n    patterns = compile_patterns(data)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE, 'r') as zin:\n        infos = zin.infolist()\n        contents = {}\n\n        total_repl = 0\n        total_cache_removed = 0\n\n        for info in infos:\n            raw = zin.read(info.filename)\n            try:\n                txt = raw.decode('utf-8')\n            except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                # Not expected in this minimal template, but keep binary entries unchanged\n                contents[info.filename] = raw\n                continue\n\n            if '{{' not in txt:\n                contents[info.filename] = raw\n                continue\n\n            new_txt, repl, cache_removed = replace_in_section_xml(txt, data, patterns)\n            total_repl += repl\n            total_cache_removed += cache_removed\n            contents[info.filename] = new_txt.encode('utf-8')\n\n    # Write output, preserving per-entry compression type and metadata as much as possible\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE, 'r') as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT, 'w') as zout:\n        for info in zin.infolist():\n            new_info = zipfile.ZipInfo(filename=info.filename, date_time=info.date_time)\n            new_info.compress_type = info.compress_type\n            new_info.comment = info.comment\n            new_info.extra = info.extra\n            new_info.internal_attr = info.internal_attr\n            new_info.external_attr = info.external_attr\n            new_info.create_system = info.create_system\n            new_info.flag_bits = info.flag_bits\n\n            data_bytes = contents[info.filename]\n            zout.writestr(new_info, data_bytes)\n\n    # Verification\n    remaining = []\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT, 'r') as z:\n        for name in z.namelist():\n            b = z.read(name)\n            try:\n                s = b.decode('utf-8')\n            except Exception:\n                continue\n            if '{{' in s:\n                tokens = re.findall(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', s)\n                remaining.append((name, tokens))\n\n        bad = z.testzip()\n        if bad:\n            raise SystemExit(f'ZIP integrity check failed at entry: {bad}')\n\n    print('Replacements (approx tokens):', total_repl)\n    print('Paragraphs with linesegarray removed:', total_cache_removed)\n\n    if remaining:\n        print('WARNING: Unreplaced placeholders found:')\n        for fp, tokens in remaining:\n            print(' ', fp, tokens)\n        raise SystemExit(2)\n\n    # Spot-check transformed fields\n    print('참석자수:', data.get('참석자수'))\n    print('만족도:', data.get('만족도'))\n    print('종합의견:', data.get('종합의견'))\n    print('Output written to', OUTPUT)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"116cef55-cce9-46b9-b39e-23d965f3d111","uuid":"24569034-606e-4147-89f0-c3fa9be8632b"}
